- **audit security remediation** (`63f99e45`, `6b740659`, `de611872`; audit SEC-001, SEC-004, SEC-212): the lockfile bumps rustls to 0.23.46 (RUSTSEC-2026-0346..0349); frontend downloads past the configured cutoff now require a matching sha256 sidecar; and the attach chrome strips bidi and zero-width characters that could spoof pane titles and roster entries.

## [0.60.0] - 2026-10-11

### Added
- **par-mux `--install-skill`: the CLI owns and installs its agent skill** (`d49b2bb`; `crates/par-mux/skills/par-mux/SKILL.md`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/skill_installer.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/bin/par_mux/main.rs`, `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_cli.rs`, `docs/MUX.md`; card 01a12b66aa39704e94a79166854654e5): the par-mux agent skill moves from the par-term repository into this one and is embedded in the binary via `include_str!`, so it can never drift from the CLI it documents; the skill content was also refreshed for the current feature set (workspaces `+N` and the workspace-prefixed `list-sessions` shape, `respawn-pane` and held-dead panes, `list-commands` capability discovery, `par-mux attach`, `--list-servers`, the config file, the full pane env contract including `PAR_MUX_CONTROL_SOCKET`, and standalone `cargo install par-mux`).
  `par-mux --install-skill [--skills-dir DIR]` writes it to `~/.claude/skills/par-mux/SKILL.md` — always overwriting, since the embedded copy is the source of truth — without starting or contacting a daemon; par-term invokes the flag after installing par-mux instead of embedding its own copy. Pinned by `install_writes_the_embedded_asset_under_the_skills_root` and `reinstall_overwrites_an_older_copy` (installer seam) and `install_skill_writes_the_bundled_skill_without_a_daemon` (end-to-end through the real binary).
- **par-mux `--install-agent-hooks` / `--install-agent-extensions`: par-mux owns its agent-state integrations** (`a22d33f`; `crates/par-mux/src/mux/integrations/`, `crates/par-mux/integrations/`, `crates/par-mux/src/bin/par_mux/main.rs`, `docs/MUX.md`; card 01a12bbe98fc706699a72dbb82d9f28a): the agent-state integrations par-term shipped — the claude/codex/grok session hooks (config-entry agents, merged into settings.json/hooks.json without disturbing user bytes, proven by round-trip tests) and the pi/omp extension files — move into this repository, embedded in the binary like the skill. Five one-shot flags drive them without contacting a daemon: `--install-agent-hooks`, `--uninstall-agent-hooks`, `--agent-hooks-status` (one `<agent> installed|absent` line per agent), `--install-agent-extensions`, `--uninstall-agent-extensions`, plus `--heal-agent-hooks` (rewrite a registered hook asset that went missing — the par-term startup self-heal, which never touches agent config); explicit clap conflict lists keep the one-shot flags mutually exclusive with `--install-skill` and the daemon-control flags. Hook assets move from par-term config directory to par-mux own (`<par-mux config dir>/hooks/`, honoring PAR_MUX_CONFIG/XDG), resolved fallibly like `--gen-config`. Safety properties audited and pinned: the scripts exit on env guards before doing anything outside a pane, the single socket write carries a 0.5 s timeout, and every failure is swallowed — an agent session start never blocks or fails because of them; the pi/omp timers are unref()ed. All 50 ported unit tests pass unchanged (strict-parse, merge round-trip, uninstall-only-ours).
  A compact atomic-save helper (sibling temp, fsync, mode-preserving rename) ports the par-term-config contract the config merges rely on, and `jsonc-parser` joins the mux feature. par-term will invoke these flags instead of embedding its own copies (companion par-term commit).


## [0.59.0] - 2026-10-10

### Added
- **par-mux passthrough: `refresh_status` adopts the client-snapshot read with legacy fallback** (`6796b36`; `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/pump.rs`; card 01a1216d4780): ENH-043 gave render mode the single-round-trip status read; passthrough still ran the five-query per-fact round every burst. When the daemon advertises client-snapshot v1, one `client-snapshot -t <window>` reply now fills every passthrough status fact (workspaces, owner session id and name, pane title, agent count); any refusal — feature absent, unknown version, error for the tracked window, transport failure — falls back to the legacy round, which also re-derives the tracked window from pane-info so a stale window id self-heals. The pane's exit state stays event-driven. Three tests pin the paths: single round trip with every fact landing, legacy round without the feature, and refusal fallback.
- **par-mux attach: the host cell pixel size is probed with XTWINOPS `CSI 16 t`** (`6780079`; `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/conn.rs`): the attach client previously reported the construction-default 10x20 cell pixels to the daemon forever, so hosts whose cells differ got panes sized for a grid that was not theirs. The OSC 11 background probe now also queries `CSI 16 t` in the same raw-mode window and parses the `CSI 6 ; h ; w t` reply (height first, then width); the probed size follows as a second `refresh-client -C … -p …`, and since the daemon's cell-size record is latest-report-wins, panes re-fit to the real metrics. A silent host keeps the 10x20 default. Seam tests cover the cell-reply parse, the 4/8-family traps, both replies in one chunk, and split reads.

### Fixed
- **par-mux passthrough: blocked host stdout writes are retried, not dropped** (`6d44741`; `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/mod.rs`; card 01a11d96654c): on a host terminal in `O_NONBLOCK` mode, a pane-byte write that would block returned `WouldBlock` and the write was dropped silently, losing the rest of the chunk. A new `write_all_blocking` helper parks 2 ms on `WouldBlock` and resumes from the bytes the terminal actually took; `Interrupted` retries too. Applied at every passthrough host stdout write — %output forwarding, the resync replay, the help dump, the respawn clear, the region restore, and the raw-mode guard's clears. A WouldBlock-once writer test proves no byte is lost.
- **par-mux passthrough: the stdin reader survives an idle nonblocking host tty** (`9c98ae1`; `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/pump.rs`; card 01a11d9687d4): with the host terminal in `O_NONBLOCK` mode, the reader thread's `read` returned `WouldBlock` whenever no bytes were pending; reporting it downward ended the reader and the pump detached the session at the first idle poll. `WouldBlock` is now the no-data-now signal — the reader parks 2 ms and retries, and `pump_stdin` treats a stray `WouldBlock` as idle instead of detach. A test drives a real nonblocking pipe through the reader: idle silence, then bytes, then EOF on close.
- **par-mux attach render mode: the no-gutter scroll cue no longer paints outside the pane** (`602fdbe`; `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/renderer.rs`; card 01a11d7e): with `scrollbar-gutter` off, a scrolled pane painted its indicator column one past its rect — a full-height theme-bg blank landing in a neighbor's first content column wherever the neighbor under-paints — while `gutter_owns` yielded the boundary cell to the scrolled pane, leaving a blank band where the divider glyph belongs. The cue is now gated strictly on `scrollbar-gutter` and the scrolled yield is gone: without a reserved gutter the pane owns all of its columns, the divider stays continuous, and the cue never occludes content the pane does not own. Pinned red-to-green by the strengthened gutter-off section of `scrollbar_gutter_option_reserves_a_column_and_indicates` (divider continuity at the boundary column).
- **fuzz: scheduled runs stop OOMing at the RSS limit** (`fad55a9`; `.github/workflows/fuzz.yml`): the ASAN quarantine grew without bound across a scheduled run, so long sessions crossed the 512 MB RSS cap and were killed; the quarantine is now bounded, ending the quarantine-churn OOM that repeatedly quarantined the kitty target. Closes blocked card "Fuzz kitty target OOMs at 512MB on schedule" (verification: green `fuzz.yml` run following this release).

## [0.58.1] - 2026-10-02

### Added
- **`check_release_notes.py` lints the checked section's structure** (ENH-048; `scripts/check_release_notes.py`): one subsection per Keep-a-Changelog type, no bullet run detached from its heading by a blank line, no `Commit <this>` placeholders, every backticked repo path exists at the head ref, and the `[Unreleased]` compare link is based on the newest versioned heading. `--write` now runs before the check so the link rule is passable in one pass, and `--no-structure` skips the rules for historical audits. `release-check` stays out of `checkall`: it blocks tagging, nothing else.
- **`check_mux_docs.py` fails when a doc claims a `--flag` clap does not define** (ENH-047; `scripts/check_mux_docs.py`): every `--flag` claimed for par-mux in `docs/MUX.md`, a `[Unreleased]` par-mux CHANGELOG bullet, or `config.rs` rustdoc must exist as a clap long flag in `crates/par-mux/src/bin/par_mux/main.rs`. Flags of other programs go in a reasoned `FOREIGN_FLAGS` allowlist, an allowlist entry that collides with a real flag fails the gate, and every extraction fails closed when it parses nothing. The self-test grew to eight injected drifts plus the empty-extraction case.
- **An `attach_input` cargo-fuzz target drives the attach client's stdin tokenizer** (ENH-046; `fuzz/fuzz_targets/attach_input.rs`, `fuzz/Cargo.toml`, `Makefile`, `.github/workflows/fuzz.yml`, `CONTRIBUTING.md`): one long-lived `InputParser` through fuzzer-chosen feed splits asserts it never panics, never emits more bytes than fed, and that a bracketed-paste body comes out only as `Token::Paste` with its bytes intact across any split. Wired into `make fuzz-all` (now eight targets) and the CI fuzz matrix.
- **par-mux `client-snapshot -t <window>`: the attach client's whole status read in one round trip** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/command/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/dispatch/client.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/status.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `docs/MUX.md`, ENH-043): one reply carries the workspaces, the sessions (the window's owner marked), the owner's windows, every pane title of the window, and the agent roster, as `snapshot 1` then `workspace`/`session`/`window`/`pane`/`agent` rows taken under one tree lock. `list-commands` advertises it as `client-snapshot v1`. The render attach client uses it when advertised, cutting a status refresh (bar, pane border labels, side panel) from up to S+P+6 queries to one, and falls back to the per-fact queries against older daemons. Additive: no existing reply or notification line changes.
- **par-mux attach render mode: the workspace side panel shows on launch (`[client] sidebar-on-launch`)** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/config.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/mod.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/session.rs`, `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_attach.rs`, `docs/MUX.md`): a new boolean client option, **default `true`**, so a render client now attaches with the side panel up. **Visible default change:** set `sidebar-on-launch = false` to keep the old hidden-at-launch behavior. The launch state is applied to the renderer before the seed's first `refresh-client -C`, so the daemon's first division already reserves the strip (no full-width layout followed by a refit), and the seed queries the workspace roster so the first frame paints the strip's rows. Prefix `s` still flips the panel at runtime; the option is read at launch only, and a config reload leaves the live panel as it is. Config-file only (no CLI flag; the attach subcommand has no boolean flags). `--gen-config` writes the key. The `mux_attach` harness now pins `PAR_MUX_CONFIG` on every attach child to a fixture-local client file (default `sidebar-on-launch = false`) so the suite never reads the developer's config. Pinned by `sidebar_on_launch_resolves_default_true_and_explicit_values`, `render_mode_sidebar_on_launch_default_shows_panel_on_first_frame` (with no chord sent, the strip's `▸` workspace row paints and the right pane's marker paints at the panel-offset column 52; red before the change), and `render_mode_sidebar_on_launch_false_keeps_panel_hidden`.
- **Two-tier Rust API: a curated `prelude` module** (`src/prelude.rs`, `src/lib.rs`, `docs/RUST_USAGE.md`, ARC-005): `par_term_emu_core_rust::prelude` re-exports the core embedder surface (Terminal, Grid, Cell, Color, Cursor, the event/observer types, `TerminalGraphic`, `TmuxNotification`, width config, and `pty_session`), with docs directing peripheral types to module-scoped paths. Every existing crate-root re-export is kept, so no consumer breaks; the `Py*` re-export wall is now `#[doc(hidden)]` (a grep found no consumer of those paths). Pinned by a prelude smoke test.
- **`ProtoConvert` derive generates streaming app↔protobuf conversions** (`derive/src/proto_convert.rs`, `src/streaming/proto.rs`, `src/streaming/proto_golden_tests.rs`, ARC-006, derive crate 0.47.0): the ENH-041-style derive approach extends to the protobuf side — `ServerMessage`/`ClientMessage` (including the complexity-43 `try_from`) plus the stats structs and `AgentEntry` now derive both conversion directions, routed through explicit `ToWire`/`FromWire` pairs (no blanket narrowing; percent/mouse-button clamps live in `proto::wire`). proto.rs shrank 1,950 → 1,359 lines; a golden test recorded from the old hand-written code proves byte-identical output across all 39 server and 11 client variants and every lossy decode path.
- **CI runs on pull_request** (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`, ARC-004): version check, lint, FFI drift, and the ubuntu/Python 3.14 test + mux jobs (attach suites included) now fire on every PR with superseded-run cancellation; the full OS×Python matrix, Windows/macOS mux, the feature matrix, `web_term/` drift, and xcframework stay on manual dispatch, and PR clippy runs the lint-check feature set (attach clippy joins after the gated render.rs decomposition lands — two pre-existing `type_complexity` errors there would redden every PR).
- **par-mux attach render mode: the side panel's ` menu ` chip opens a command menu** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/mod.rs`, `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_attach.rs`, `docs/MUX.md`): the panel footer's ` menu ` chip now pops a small themed command menu in the context-menu machinery — `keybinds` (opens the keybinds/help panel), `reload config` (the reload chord's client rebind plus the daemon's `reload-config`), and `detach` (ends the client exactly like prefix `d`, exit 0) — with mouse-selectable rows mapped by the raw host column like the other menus. Before, the chip opened the active workspace's menu, which stays reachable by a right press on a workspace row; the chip no longer depends on a loaded workspace roster. ` new ` keeps opening the new-workspace prompt. The keybinds panel's mouse section lists both chips. Pinned by `compose_command_menu_maps_rows_to_actions`, `menu_chip_opens_the_command_menu`, the per-row `command_menu_*` click tests, and end to end by `render_mode_panel_menu_chip_detaches`.
- **par-mux client lifecycle notifications: `%client-attached` / `%client-left`** (`crates/par-term-emu-core/src/tmux_control.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/emit.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/server/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/mod.rs`, `src/python_bindings/types/notification.rs`, `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_daemon.rs`, `docs/MUX.md`, `docs/API_REFERENCE.md`): the daemon had no client lifecycle signal — tmux's `%client-detached` was parse-only and never emitted, and nothing announced a new client. A control client's registration (its first control command; hook-only connections never register) now broadcasts `%client-attached <id>` to the clients already registered — never to the joining client, so the registration-replay-before-first-reply ordering is unchanged — and every registered connection's end broadcasts `%client-left <id> [$S @W]`, carrying the session and window it was displaying when it had reported a view (`refresh-client -C`). The leave rides the single connection-teardown site, so a clean disconnect, a socket EOF, and an eviction teardown all emit it, queued after the `%layout-change` of any window the departure re-fit (layout-then-lifecycle, dispatch's order). `client` is the daemon's decimal connection id; `%client-detached` stays parse-only. The attach render and passthrough clients mark their status chrome dirty on both. **Rust API:** `TmuxNotification` gains `ClientAttached { client }` and `ClientLeft { client, session_id, window_id }` — exhaustive matches over the (non-`#[non_exhaustive]`) enum need the two arms; Python `TmuxNotification.notification_type` gains `client-attached`/`client-left`. `list-clients` (a client-count query) is not added. Pinned red-to-green by the two-real-client `client_attach_and_drop_broadcast_lifecycle_lines` plus an emit round-trip; the eviction path is covered by construction (it ends in the same teardown), not by a dedicated eviction test.
- **par-mux multi-client sync: smallest-attached-client window sizing + shared-selection notifications** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/tree/mod.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/tree/layout_ops.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/dispatch/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/server/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/emit.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_daemon.rs`, `docs/MUX.md`): `refresh-client -C` now records the connection's sizing contribution — its reported grid and the window it displays — and a window's extent is the componentwise minimum over the clients currently displaying it. A plain control or hook connection that never reports a size holds no contribution; a constraining client's disconnect (or a view switch) re-fits the window to the remaining viewers' minimum, a window nobody displays keeps its extent until next displayed, and repeated identical reports are idempotent (the re-fit fires only on an extent change, so the reports reseeds and handshakes replay cannot feed back into a resize loop). Embedder/test dispatches without a connection id keep the legacy direct-resize behavior, and a streaming mirror viewer's `Resize` is now a sizing contribution under the same rule (previously latest-report-wins — the last report resized the window outright). Two selection notifications make multi-client sync real: `select-window` on the displayed session now emits `%session-window-changed $N @N` (previously parser-only, never emitted — B never learned A's tab switch), and `select-workspace` adds `%client-session-changed <workspace> $N <name>` when the displayed session moved (the `client` field carries the workspace, par-mux has no client names). The attach renderer parks these in its event handler and performs the follow on the pump with queries and a reseed — it never re-selects, so the broadcast cannot echo into a loop — an external `%window-pane-changed` on the shown window re-points the local focus highlight, and a reseed clears the parked follow state (no stale events). Pinned by five two-real-client daemon tests (minimum sizing stable under repeated reports, disconnect grows the window, tab/workspace switch broadcast, unreported control client never constrains, and the followed window's screen reconstructed in a real `Terminal` from the resync replay) plus five tree-level rule tests and two emit round-trips.
- **par-mux attach render mode round 8: workspaces-only selection follows clicks, bare-name tabs, menu row mapping, raw rename names** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/tabs.rs`, `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_attach.rs`, `docs/MUX.md`): clicking a workspace row now SELECTS it daemon-side (`land_on_workspace` sends `select-workspace` before the landing) — the click used to re-seed the view while the daemon's active-workspace pointer stayed put, leaving the side panel's highlight on the old workspace (the manual-pass report; the keybind path already selected). Tab strips render the bare NAME — the `N:` ordinal prefix is gone, matching the reference rendering (every strip unit/integration pin updated to the narrower tabs). The context menu's click mapping drops an off-by-one: the overlay paints at buffer rows rebased +1 host row by the frame, so panel row r sits at host y0+2+r — the old mapping read every click one row low and fired the neighboring action (pinned by the menu-click tests moved to true geometry; the picker's clicks inherit the same fix). And `rename-window` commits send the RAW name: the daemon grammar takes the rest of the line verbatim, so the wire quoting added in round 6 became literal quotes in tab names (the value-flag commits — `select-pane -T`, `new-window -n`, `rename-workspace`, `new-workspace -n` — keep their quoting; their flags parse it). And the restart CLI test helper drains both piped streams while waiting — a pipe fills at 64 KB and the parent half's stderr announcements once stalled behind an undrained pipe, deadlocking `wait_exited` (the round-8 gate).
- **par-mux daemon: `new-workspace` spawns the workspace's first session** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/dispatch/`, `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_daemon.rs`, `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_restart.rs`): creating a workspace no longer leaves an empty roster row the attach client's view can never reach — `new-workspace` now also spawns the workspace's FIRST session/window (named after the workspace, the default shell) through the same two-phase spawn every session rides, so the side panel's ` new ` chip lands the view on a usable workspace the moment it exists (the manual-pass round-7 report: the created workspace highlighted in the side bar while the demo tabs stayed up and nothing could switch). The wire reply stays the workspace id; `%workspaces-changed` and `%sessions-changed` both ride the create, and the restart restore carries the auto-spawned session (the restart test's exact-id asserts moved to workspace+name pairing). The keybinds panel's categories reordered to `global, workspaces, tabs/windows/sessions, panes, navigation, mouse` (the manual-pass ask).
- **Standalone par-mux release binaries on GitHub Releases** (`.github/workflows/deployment.yml`, `scripts/package_mux_release.py`, `Makefile`, `docs/MUX.md`, `README.md`): the release workflow gains a `build-mux-binaries` job — five `par-mux` daemon targets (Linux x86_64/ARM64 native on `ubuntu-24.04-arm`, macOS ARM64 native and x86_64 cross on `macos-latest`, Windows x86_64) built `--locked --release --no-default-features --features rust-only,mux-bin,attach --bin par-mux` (no Python, no par-term dependency; the `mux` → `serde` closure keeps tree persistence compiled in). Native targets smoke-test `--version`/`--help`; the macOS x86_64 cross build is never executed (Rosetta not assumed). `scripts/package_mux_release.py` (stdlib-only, `make mux-package-check` self-test added to `checkall`) stages the executable + `LICENSE` + a generated install note and archives `par-mux-v<version>-<target>.tar.gz`/`.zip` with normalized mtimes and a preserved 0755 exec bit, plus a per-target sha256 file the `github-release` job merges into `par-mux-v<version>-SHA256SUMS.txt`; archives upload under the `mux-binaries-*` artifact pattern. Install docs (extract → copy into `PATH`; Windows adds a `PATH` directory) live in docs/MUX.md and README.md.
- **par-mux attach render mode round 6: coherent panel-up top row, workspaces-only side panel, context menus, right-aligned Help chip** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/tabs.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/status.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/input.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/mod.rs`, `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_attach.rs`, `docs/MUX.md`): the round-5 build shipped a real click defect — `route_mouse` rebased the host column by the side panel's width BEFORE the tab-strip branch, so with the panel up every top-row click mapped one panel width left and the ` + ` button was unclickable. Round 6 makes the geometry coherent instead: with the panel up the TAB STRIP paints the panel's ` workspaces ` title accent in the panel's columns (shared row 0, the design mock's header), lays the tabs out from them onward with ` + ` still pinned at the strip's right edge, and the hit-test takes the RAW host column through that offset (lead columns hit nothing) — pinned red-to-green by `render_mode_plus_click_works_with_the_panel_up` and at the unit level by `lead_segment_offsets_the_tabs_and_keeps_the_plus_pinned`. The side panel lists WORKSPACES ONLY (the nested `win:` rows and their dispatch arm are gone; `render_mode_workspace_click_reseeds_with_the_panel` moved its click up a row with the header) and drops its section header rows — the title moved to the strip's lead segment — while a new footer row pins ` new ` (the new-workspace prompt, default the next non-conflicting workspace index via `next_workspace_name`, commit sending `new-workspace -n <quoted name>` then `select-workspace` + landing the view on the fresh workspace) and ` menu ` (the ACTIVE workspace's menu) to the panel's bottom-left/bottom-right, each chip its own clickable span and id (`panel:new`/`panel:menu`). Context menus arrive: a RIGHT press on a tab opens that window's menu and a right press on a workspace row (or the ` menu ` chip) opens the workspace menu — both a small modal in the picker's overlay machinery (`rename` / `close` / `add tab`|`new`, esc/q closes, a click on an action row dispatches, elsewhere consumes; the pure row→action mapping pinned by `compose_menu_panel_maps_rows_to_actions`, the right-press decode by `right_press_decodes_and_rejects_its_lookalikes`). Rename targets carry their id (`PromptTarget::Window(+id)`/`PromptTarget::Workspace(+id)`), so renaming from a menu hits the CLICKED window, not the shown one, and every prompt commit now quotes its name through `wire_quote` — including the pre-existing `select-pane -T` and `rename-window` paths (closes the unquoted-names follow-up card). Kills recover: closing the SHOWN window lands the view on its session's surviving daemon-active (or first) window, closing the shown workspace lands on the first surviving workspace, and none remaining ends the view cleanly through the refresh's SessionGone. And the status bar's ` C-b? Help ` chip RIGHT-ALIGNS at the row's right edge — the reservation's width math unchanged, the paint now clips the content at the chip's left column so a full line can never push the chip off the row (pinned by `paint_right_aligns_the_chip_and_clips_content_at_it`).
- **par-mux attach render mode round 5: refit strip repaint, herdr default, status-bar chrome, tab `+`/new-tab prompt, help scrollbar** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/status.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/tabs.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/mod.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/config.rs`, `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_attach.rs`, `docs/MUX.md`): the refit's full repaint (`ESC[2J`) cleared the tab-strip and status rows too, but both rows flush diff-based and nothing invalidated them on the sidebar-toggle refit path — part of the status bar (and the whole tab strip) stayed wiped after bringing the panel up (pinned by `render_mode_sidebar_toggle_repaints_the_strip_rows`, red-proofed against the fix). `herdr` (per-pane boxes) is now the default border style — `[client] border-lines` defaults to `herdr`, so the seed paints per-pane rounded boxes and the `pane-borders` paint mode follows the style's own default (pinned by the rewritten `render_mode_border_style_survives_split`: seed `╭`, a full cycle returns, and the chord split keeps them). The status bar ends with a ` C-b? Help ` chip — the live help keybind the way the keybinds panel spells it, its width reserved before compose so it survives truncation (unit-pinned by `help_chip_spells_the_live_keybind`); while hidden the bottom row is never flushed at all, so the grown pane bottom row can never be erased (pinned by `hidden_status_bar_flushes_nothing`). Prefix `S` (`[client] status-bar`) toggles the bottom bar — hidden by default-shown, the pane grid grows the row, and the bar's vanishing is its own feedback (a flash would paint onto the hidden row; pinned by `render_mode_status_bar_toggle_hides_and_restores_the_row` via the cursor's host-row shift both ways). The tab strip's ` + ` button owns a reserved three-column slot at the strip's right edge (the tab area truncates beside it, so the button never disappears on crowded strips): a click opens herdr's new-tab prompt (` new tab ` title, `enter save · ^c clear · esc cancel` footer, `^C` clearing the input) seeded with the next non-conflicting window index (bumped past any window NAME claiming the number; unit-pinned by `next_window_name_bumps_past_conflicts`), Enter sending `new-window -t <session> -n <name>` and landing the view on the fresh window (end to end: `render_mode_plus_click_prompts_and_creates_a_window`). And the keybinds modal paints a `┃` scroll thumb on the ring's right column when the content overflows (positioned from the scroll state, pinned by `help_overlay_paints_a_scrollbar_thumb_when_it_overflows`), while the idle help and picker panels drop the `press / to filter` placeholder row — the footers already advertise `search /` and `filter /`; the filter line still appears the instant `/` opens it (compose and renderer tests updated).
- **par-mux attach render mode: sidebar re-seed and border-cycle defects from the manual pass** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_attach.rs`): the border-cycle chord flips the session-level `pane-borders` flag now, not just the renderer's — the chord changed the renderer's copy only, so the split chord's re-seed restored the session flag and every cycled-to herdr box set reverted to shared dividers on the first split (pinned by `render_mode_border_style_survives_split`, which splits through the chord). Re-seeds rebuild the renderer at the HOST grid and report the fresh `window_size` — the re-seed used to construct the renderer at the REPORTED width (grid less the side panel), ratcheting the view one panel-width narrower on every re-seed while the panel was up, each report then shrinking again (the click-a-workspace-row-shrinks-the-panes report; pinned by the two-click geometry asserts in `render_mode_workspace_click_reseeds_with_the_panel`). And the panel's nested window rows carry a child indent so a window row can no longer be clicked as if it were a second workspace row.
- **par-mux attach render mode: three sidebar/label/border defects from the manual pass** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/status.rs`, `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_attach.rs`): pane content no longer paints the side panel's width twice — the content inset (`content_view`) silently carried the strip offset because `display_rect` already applied it, so with the strip up every pane's content shifted right twice (clipped at the frame edge, dead margin between the divider and the pushed content) while dividers and borders moved once (pinned at the renderer level by `sidebar_offset_paints_content_and_dividers_together`, end to end at 41→51→41 columns across the toggle by `render_mode_sidebar_toggle_refits_pane_geometry`). Every re-seed path now re-applies the session's chosen border-glyph set and the panel width/rows to the fresh renderer — splitting no longer reverts a cycled-to herdr border set to plain Unicode singles, and a panel-row click's re-seed no longer blanks the panel (pinned by `render_mode_border_style_survives_split` and `render_mode_workspace_click_reseeds_with_the_panel`). And in the strip-up top row, the solid shaded block moved from the active window's tab to the active workspace's label (the active tab is the bold run instead) — with both shaded, the line read as selecting a different workspace than the panel highlighted (pinned by the extended `compose_sidebar_mode_leads_with_the_active_workspace`).
- **par-mux side panel refinements from the manual pass** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/status.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/mod.rs`): the sidebar nests each workspace's sessions' windows dim beneath its row (herdr's tabs-under-workspace look) with prefixed row ids — a workspace-row click lands on the workspace, a window-row click selects that window — and the ACTIVE workspace paints as herdr's full-width inverted block (not just a brighter glyph run); with the strip up, the top status row switches to herdr's compact form, leading with the active workspace's label before the tabs instead of the all-workspaces/sessions rosters the strip now carries. Also fixes a leftover debug probe that printed raw `list-workspaces` replies into the focused pane on every sidebar refresh.
- **par-mux attach render mode: toggleable workspace side panel** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/mod.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/config.rs`, `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_attach.rs`): prefix `s` (`[client] sidebar`, width `[client] sidebar-width`, default 20 clamped 6–60) toggles herdr's left strip — themed-bg fill, dim right-edge divider, sections composed top-down by the pure `compose_sidebar` (the queried workspace roster first: accent header, `▸`-marked active row, click-to-land through the shared select+resync; built to take more sections). While up, the client reports the reduced grid (`window_size` minus the strip) so the daemon re-divides around it, every pane/border/divider/cursor/mouse coordinate maps through the strip offset (the paint clamp bounds the pre-broadcast frame), the roster re-queries on open and with every throttled status refresh, and toggling off reports the full width back. Pinned by `render_mode_sidebar_toggle_minimal` (toggle on: flash + strip section + reduced-division re-seed; toggle off: restore) and `compose_sidebar_renders_sections_and_marks_active`.
- **par-mux attach render mode: workspace picker, scroll-position indicator, default-on pane labels, modal footer band, and shift-arrow feedback** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/mod.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/config.rs`, `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_attach.rs`): prefix `g` (`[client] workspace-picker`) opens the session/window picker's workspace sibling — the daemon's workspace roster in the same themed modal (filter, cursor, clicks, dark-grey footer band), Enter landing through the same select-then-resync; a scrolled pane shows a `▐` position marker in its right edge even without `scrollbar-gutter` (the divider yields the column while it shows); `show-label-in-border` now defaults ON — pane titles show in their borders in the per-pane-box modes, prefix `l` still toggles; the help/picker/prompt modals' controls footers paint on a dark-grey band; and the shift+arrow swap flashes `swapped …` (or `no pane in that direction` at an edge) and matches the shift bit loosely, so a terminal co-reporting modifiers still swaps (the manual-pass "prefix+S arrows does not work" report). Pinned by the extended `render_mode_zoom_rename_border_chords` (workspace picker open/dismiss, label toggle polarity) and the compose/overlay units.
- **par-mux attach render mode: herdr border style, pane-label toggle, directional swap, and a herdr-shaded tab strip** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/status.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/mod.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/config.rs`, `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_attach.rs`): the border cycle gains a fifth style, `herdr` — every pane draws its own complete rounded box (the style owns the paint mode; an explicit `pane-borders` still overrides for any glyph set), selectable live with prefix `B` or `[client] border-lines = "herdr"`; prefix `l` toggles each pane's title embedded in its border (`[client] label-toggle`, default `l`; labels paint in the per-pane-box modes); Shift+arrows swap the focused pane with the pane in that direction (`swap-pane`, focus follows the content, at an edge nothing moves); and the status row's workspace/window tabs render herdr-style — the active tab a solid shaded block, the rest dim with `|` separators (the `*` marker and `{window}:` prefixes are gone; the tabs are names). Pinned by the extended `render_mode_zoom_rename_border_chords`, `compose_shades_the_active_workspace_only`, and `compose_bolds_the_current_session_and_shades_the_active_window`.
- **par-mux `resize-pane` resizes on both axes** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/tree/layout_ops.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/tree/tests.rs`): the relative form now walks to the nearest same-axis ancestor split at any depth (tmux semantics — the extent walk `set_leaf_extent` already had), so a pane nested under a cross-orientation split (say a stacked pair inside a side-by-side root) resizes with `-U`/`-D` AND `-L`/`-R`; before, only the pane's own bordering split answered and the other axis errored `PaneNotResizable`. Wire semantics unchanged (`-R`/`-D` grow the pane, `-L`/`-U` shrink), lone panes and single-axis windows still error. Pinned by `resize_pane_reaches_a_cross_orientation_ancestor`.
- **par-mux attach render mode: zoom, rename, and border-style chords** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/mod.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/config.rs`, `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_attach.rs`): `prefix z` rides the daemon's existing `resize-pane -Z` — the focused pane's PTY takes the full window grid, the `%layout-change` re-seed repaints it, select-away unzooms (all the daemon's zoom rules apply), and a bold ` Z ` cue marks the status row; `,` opens a modal rename prompt (seeded with the current name; Enter commits as `rename-window`, Escape cancels); `$` is the pane-title variant (`select-pane -T`); `B` cycles the divider/border glyphs unicode → double → heavy → ascii, live; prefix+arrow keys select the nearest pane in the arrow's direction (while zoomed they aim through the cached tree geometry, the daemon unzooms on the select). All four chords rebind via `[client] zoom` / `rename-window` / `rename-pane` / `border-cycle`, live on reload; the startup glyph set is `[client] border-lines` (`unicode|double|heavy|ascii`, default `unicode`). Passthrough consumes the four chords unbound (render-only). Pinned by `render_mode_zoom_rename_border_chords` (`crates/par-mux/tests/mux_attach.rs`), `pane_in_direction_picks_the_nearest_pane_in_the_direction` and the help-rows update (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/mod.rs`).
- **par-mux split-window/new-window tmux target parity** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/ids.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/command/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/dispatch/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/tree/`): `split-window -t` accepts `$N`/`@N`/name in addition to `%N` (window → its active pane, session → its active window's active pane); `new-window -t @N`/`%N` inserts the new window immediately after that window (active index follows the shift). Pinned by `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_targets.rs` and tree tests. Landed from a parallel worktree agent's branch, squash-merged.
- **par-mux gate: the attach feature joins every mux gate** (`Makefile`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`): the mux suite and CI clippy never enabled the `attach` feature, so the attach client's lib tests and the `mux_attach` PTY suite compiled in NO gate — the horizontal-divider regression (below) shipped through three green gates that way. `attach` now rides the mux suite line and the clippy line (CI and local).
- **par-mux `[daemon] exit-empty` toggle** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/config.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/server/`, `docs/MUX.md`): the exit-when-empty shutdown is now configurable — `exit-empty = false` holds a persisting daemon past its grace however long it sits empty; the default `true` is the shipped behavior. Second live daemon setting: `reload-config` (or the `C-b C-r` chord) applies a changed value without a restart. gen-config emits the row. Pinned by `exit_empty_false_keeps_an_empty_persisting_server_alive` (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/server/`), `reload_applies_exit_empty_live` and `exit_empty_defaults_to_on` (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/config.rs`).
- **par-mux `attach` render mode: herdr-parity display options — `pane-gaps`, `scrollbar-gutter`, `drag-cursor-shape`** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/config.rs`): three independent `[client]` options, ALL DEFAULT OFF (the current look renders byte-identical, pinned by test). `pane-gaps` (cells, default 0) insets each pane's rect per side so theme-bg gap bands separate the panes (content, borders, labels, and cursor mapping follow; the boundary dividers keep their geometry); `scrollbar-gutter` reserves a right-edge gutter column per pane rect (content narrows by one; a minimal scroll-position indicator shows while the pane's client scrollback view is scrolled, and the boundary divider yields the gutter column); `drag-cursor-shape` shapes the host cursor to the resize shape (best-effort DECSCUSR steady block) while a divider drag is live and restores on drag end. gen-config emits all three; the reload chord rebinds them live.
- **par-mux `attach` render mode: session/window picker modal on prefix `w`** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/mod.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/config.rs`): `[client] picker` (default `w`) opens a herdr-style picker over the frame reusing the help panel's modal chrome (theme bg, accent ring, `esc close` badge, footer controls line) — sessions with their windows nested (`>` marks the current session/window, `*` the session's active window), a `/` filter-as-you-type box, arrows/`j`/`k` selection, Enter or click select (select-window + resync; a session header lands on its active window), esc/`q` dismiss; the help panel lists the chord at its effective binding, gen-config emits the key, and the reload chord rebinds it live. The `W`/`C-w` workspace chords and `?` are unchanged.
- **High-risk adversarial test coverage for the new mux surfaces** (test-only except where noted; `fuzz/corpus/mux_parse_command/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/command/` (test), `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/input.rs` tests, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/discovery.rs` tests, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/config.rs` tests, `tests/test_notification_workspace.py` new): ten fuzz corpus seeds for the workspace-era wire commands (`new-workspace`, `list-workspaces`, `select-workspace`/`rename-workspace`/`kill-workspace` `-t +N`, `list-sessions -t +N`, `reload-config`, `list-windows -t $N`, `list-panes -t @N`) plus a corpus regression test that drives every seed through `parse_command`/`parse_line` where cargo-fuzz is not run; an adversarial suite for the render client's `InputParser` (byte-at-a-time decoding, every byte-boundary split of a paste marker, hostile paste bodies, bursts interleaving paste/chord/plain runs, and the unterminated-paste cap); discovery-registry corruption coverage (garbage/truncated/wrong-typed/empty/1MB registry files are skipped without panic, a hostile socket entry probes dead and is pruned); hostile config-file coverage (wrong types, unknown keys, a 1MB value, invalid UTF-8, duplicate keys, a directory-shaped path — classified errors, never panics, plus a gen-config round-trip); and a Python binding test pinning `%workspaces-changed` through `drain_tmux_notifications`.
- **par-mux `attach`: workspaces surface — status segment, `W`/`C-w` switching chords, and `%workspaces-changed` staleness** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/status.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/mod.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/config.rs`)
- **par-mux `[daemon] remain-on-exit` toggle, defaulted to auto-remove** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/config.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/server/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/persist.rs`)
- **par-mux `attach --mode render`: clickable tab strip across the top row (par-term style)** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/tabs.rs` new, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/mod.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/status.rs`, `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_attach.rs`, `docs/MUX.md`; feature `attach`): the render-mode frame gains a one-row TAB STRIP at the host's top row — one tab per window of the shown session (`N:name`, window order), the active tab in the theme accent (indexed 14, bold), the inactive tabs dim. The strip reads the SAME queried state as the status bar (the `list-windows -t` half of the throttled re-query), so a window add/close/rename (`%sessions-changed` family) refreshes it with the status row, and it follows the same redraw discipline (invalidate on seed/resize/re-seed, diff-based flush so churn repaints only changed cells). Truncation keeps the active tab always visible (the par-term tab-bar rule, `/Users/probello/Repos/par-term/src/tab_bar_ui/tab_painter.rs`): full names when they fit; otherwise every name truncated to the widest shared budget with an ellipsis; otherwise a contiguous run of tabs containing the active one, `…` markers at each hidden edge. A click on a tab hits the painted layout (paint and hit-testing share one `layout_tabs` function, so what a click highlights is what painted) and switches to that window through the existing select+resync contract (`select-window -t` + full re-seed) — a strip click never focuses or forwards into a pane, drags never start on the strip row, and an in-flight divider drag continues when the pointer wanders onto the strip. The content height arithmetic accounts for the strip everywhere the status row already did: the renderer is built at rows−2 (strip + status), the pump's resize re-fit reports `refresh-client -C cols x (rows−2)`, the pane diff and cursor placement rebase one host row down, the strip flushes at host row 0, and the help modal centers within the shrunk content area. Frame painting is now bounds-safe against a stale layout racing a host shrink (a rect past the buffer edge clips instead of panicking). Pinned by the `tabs::` unit suite (10 tests: placement, shared-budget truncation, active-visible runs, hit-testing, accent/dim painting, diff discipline) and, in `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `session_height_carves_the_strip_and_status_out`, `frame_flushes_the_strip_at_row_zero_and_rebases_the_panes`, and `strip_click_switches_windows_without_forwarding` (wire: `select-window` + the 80x23 content size report, no pane traffic); PTY-level by `render_mode_tab_click_switches_the_active_window` (`crates/par-mux/tests/mux_attach.rs`, the daemon's active-window marker moves). The mouse-forward and host-resize pins updated to the strip-shifted coordinates (`0;10;4M` pane-relative row, 100x28 content grid). Commit `4c90a50`.
- **par-mux `attach` render mode: per-pane border boxes and border labels (default off)** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/config.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `docs/MUX.md`; feature `attach`, drawing on herdr's pane chrome in `~/Repos/herdr/src/ui/panes.rs`): `[client] pane-borders = true` replaces the shared-divider look with a complete box-drawing ring per pane — the focused pane's border bright-cyan bold, the rest dim, content inset by the border cells (the cursor placement maps through the same inset) — and `[client] show-label-in-border = true` embeds each pane's user title in its top edge, space-padded and truncated to fit (label cells are not drag handles; the plain segments stay draggable). Both default off, live-rebindable on the reload chord, rendered by `gen-config`. Pinned by `pane_borders_option_replaces_dividers_with_full_boxes` and `label_in_border_embeds_title_and_blocks_drag_on_label_cells` (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`). Commit `3d4ecea`.
- **par-mux `attach` render mode round 3: probe, re-seed, cursor, drag, and help-panel fixes** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/conn.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/input.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/mod.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/config.rs`, `docs/MUX.md`; feature `attach`): (1) **OSC 11 probe**: the black fallback on a failed probe is GONE — a failed probe leaves the frame fill at the terminal default (no painted color), so it can never mismatch the theme; default-to-black only renders when the probe SUCCEEDED with black. The probe's own parser had the field bug: 4-hex-digit components (Ghostty's 16-bit `rgb:1e1e/…` replies) shift-overflowed `1u16 << 16` and produced garbage/black — fixed in u32 and pinned by `osc_color_report_parses_and_yields_gracefully`. The race audit: the probe and the pump's stdin reader never overlap (the reader is born after the probe returns); a reply that lands after the 150 ms deadline is swallowed whole by the input parser (`late_osc11_reply_is_dropped_not_forwarded`) instead of fragmenting into the pane. (2) **Re-seed bg loss**: `prefix n`/`p` (and resize re-fit) rebuilt the renderer, silently dropping the probed background back to black — the session now keeps the resolved bg and re-applies it across every reconstruction (pinned by the fill tests). (3) **Cursor off-by-one**: `replay_all_panes` appended an extra `\n` to the re-joined replay body, driving the freshly positioned cursor one row below the shell prompt — the join is now exact (pinned by `focused_cursor_maps_through_the_rect_and_the_border_inset`). (4) **Divider drag starvation**: the second motion event destroyed the active drag (`take()` on an Active state), so the resize fired once at most while the highlight stayed up — promotion now only applies to a PENDING press (pinned by `drag_survives_active_motion_and_resizes_per_cell`). (5) **Help panel**: the modal paints the resolved theme bg on every cell (no more default/light cells), a rounded box-drawing ring in the accent with the `keybinds` title and `esc close` badge embedded in the top border, the filter line is ALWAYS visible (placeholder when inactive, ` /query▌` cursor the moment `/` is pressed), and with the panel open wheels scroll the PANEL instead of falling through to the pane (pinned by `help_modal_paints_theme_bg_accent_ring_and_title`, `compose_help_panel_filters_scrolls_and_chromes`, and `help_open_consumes_wheel_events`). Commit `3d4ecea`.
- **par-mux `attach`: pane resize affordances — prefix+arrow resize chords and divider mouse-drag** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/config.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/mod.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/conn.rs`, `Cargo.toml`, `docs/MUX.md`; feature `attach`): splits can now be resized from a live attach, on top of the daemon wire's existing `resize-pane` forms. **Resize chords** (both modes): prefix `R` (settable as `[client] resize`, tmux spelling or literal through the shared prefix grammar) enters a sticky resize mode — arrows send the wire's relative `resize-pane -t <focused> -L|-R|-U|-D <step>` at the configured step (`[client] resize-step`, default 1, floored at 1) until Enter/Escape/`q` exits; any other key leaves the mode and is reprocessed by the normal router (in passthrough it forwards, chords still need the prefix; in render mode it is consumed). The mode entry flashes a cue on the status row/flash path; the step rebinds live on the reload chord. **Divider mouse-drag** (render mode): a press within one cell of a divider starts a pending drag — it never focuses or forwards (a divider press is not a click-through), motion promotes it and maps the pointer's cell delta along the divider's axis to the same relative `resize-pane` aimed at the boundary's left/top pane, applied one cell per unapplied delta at the frame cadence (the `%layout-change` the resize queues re-seeds the window through the existing pending-layout path), and the dragged divider renders reversed while the drag lasts; a release without motion falls through as a click (the pane under the pointer still focuses, `select-pane` rides the wire, and the release forwards when the pane owns the mouse). **Swap chords:** prefix `{`/`}` (settable as `[client] swap-prev`/`swap-next`, tmux's spellings) send `swap-pane -s <focused> -t <neighbor>` from the layout-order roster (`swap-pane -s %0 -t %1` for a two-pane window's prev wrap); render mode lets the swap's `%layout-change` re-seed the window. **Help panel:** prefix `?` (settable as `[client] help`) opens the bindings modal — a bordered panel titled `keybinds` with an `esc close` badge and a footer controls line (`search / · scroll j/k/arrows/pgup/pgdn · close esc/enter`); entries group into category headers (`global`, `panes`, `windows/sessions`, **`navigation`**, `mouse`) with each row showing the chord at its EFFECTIVE binding (a remapped chord shows its remapped key), `/` opens a filter-as-you-type box (Backspace edits, Enter commits), j/k/arrows/PgUp/PgDn scroll the content window, esc/Enter/q close and the prior frame repaints; keys never reach the pane while it is up. Passthrough prints the same category rows as plain text. **Host background probe:** the renderer's frame fill now probes the host with OSC 11 (`ESC ] 11 ; ? ST`, poll(2)-based, 150 ms deadline, unix-only; black stays the fallback) right after raw mode comes up and before the pump's stdin reader starts, so a dark-grey host theme no longer paints black frames, and a corrected `set-client-colors` rides the probe. **Focus indicator side-naming** (manual-pass item 2): each divider boundary names its side — bright cyan when the focused pane is the boundary's left/top pane, bright magenta when it is the right/bottom pane — so a focus switch flips the shared two-pane divider's color visibly (a single accent read identically from either side). All new chords and the step are live-rebindable on the reload chord. Pinned by `resize_chord_arrows_send_resize_pane_and_q_exits`, `resize_mode_cancelling_key_reprocesses_normally`, `resize_chord_and_step_follow_the_config`, `swap_prev_chord_sends_swap_pane`, `help_rows_carry_effective_bindings_and_categories`, and `compose_help_panel_filters_scrolls_and_chromes` (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/mod.rs`); `drag_on_divider_resizes_without_clicking_through`, `click_near_divider_without_drag_still_focuses`, `focus_flip_changes_the_shared_dividers_color`, `render_resize_chord_sends_for_the_focused_pane`, and `help_chord_opens_filters_and_dismissal_restores_the_frame` (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`); and `resize_chord_and_step_parse_with_the_step_floored` (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/config.rs`). The btop-exit stale-cell artifact (manual-pass item 4) was NOT reproduced headlessly in this round; the repro shape is an alt-screen session with styled cells fed to a pane emulator, exit via `1049l`, re-seed, and check the renderer diff for stale bg — queued for the next pass. Commit `b64740f`.
- **par-mux `attach` render mode: tmux-convention divider halves and configurable border colors** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/config.rs`, `docs/MUX.md`): a shared divider now highlights only the HALF nearer the active pane — a side-by-side split's divider highlights its top half while the left pane is focused and its bottom half while the right one is; horizontal dividers mirror it (the whole-divider cyan/magenta side-naming is gone). The highlight and the unfocused look are configurable via `[client] border-active-color` / `border-color` (`#rrggbb` hex, defaults bright-cyan-accent / dim), applied at attach and live on the reload chord, emitted by gen-config. Pinned by the rewritten `focus_flip_changes_the_shared_dividers_color` and `focus_highlights_adjacent_dividers` (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`) and the config round-trip.
- **par-mux `attach`: render-mode resize arrows follow the divider, and the modal hides the cursor** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`): resize-mode arrows move the SHARED divider in the pressed direction from either side — a focused pane on the far side of its boundary inverts the wire direction (growing the right pane used to move the divider left; the manual-pass report) — and a modal overlay (help, picker) now hides the host cursor instead of drawing the pane's cursor through the panel, restoring placement on dismissal. Pinned by the extended `render_resize_chord_sends_for_the_focused_pane` and `modal_overlay_hides_the_pane_cursor` (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`).
- **par-mux `attach` passthrough: the status bar redraws itself** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/mod.rs`): the bar was drawn once per state change, so a full-screen pane app that wiped the reserved row (or reset the host's scroll margins) left it gone for the rest of the session (the manual-pass htop report). The pump now redraws it every ~0.5 s when nothing else did — one small absolute-CUP run, self-healing under any TUI.
- **par-mux daemon discovery: `--list-servers` and the attach multi-server guard** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/discovery.rs` new, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/mod.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/bin/par_mux/main.rs`, `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_cli.rs`, `docs/MUX.md`): `par-mux --list-servers` enumerates every par-mux daemon this user can reach, so attaching no longer requires remembering socket paths. Each daemon writes a pointer file into its state directory at bind (`<state-base>/servers/<socket-stem>.json`: full socket path, pid, start timestamp, build stamp — tmp-then-rename atomic, re-registration over a live same-socket entry is a byte-for-byte no-op, a dead or foreign-socket entry is replaced) and removes it on the clean shutdown path after the final state save; a crash's stale entry is pruned by the next enumeration, which probes every registered socket (plus — only when the base is the platform default, keeping `--state-dir`-scoped runs hermetic — the named-default socket directory, so named daemons an unregistered binary serves still appear) with `version` + `list-sessions` through the shared `MuxClient` transport and prints one line per live server: `<name-or-path>  sessions=N  <stamp>`, pruning reported on stderr. `par-mux attach` with no NAME and no `--socket` while multiple servers are live refuses with that list plus a `attach to one with par-mux attach <name|path>` hint (exit 1) instead of silently picking one; exactly one live server is attached directly (the resolved socket is pre-set on `AttachOptions`, so the attach module needs no change). Windows named-pipe friendliness comes free: the registry is plain files, the probe rides `connect_local_stream`, and nothing in discovery is unix-only. Pinned by the registry unit suite in `crates/par-mux/src/mux/discovery.rs` (8 tests: pid liveness, stem-keyed registration, live no-op, dead and foreign-socket replacement, unregister matching, name classification, display labels) and the live-daemon CLI tests in `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_cli.rs` (`list_servers_shows_both_running_daemons_with_session_counts`: two daemons, one list, session counts, clean-shutdown removal, kill-path pruning; `list_servers_prunes_only_dead_entries_and_reregistration_is_a_noop`; `list_servers_with_nothing_running_reports_empty`; `attach_with_multiple_live_servers_refuses_with_a_list`). Commit `b6ef14b`.
- **par-mux `attach`: window/pane management chords — split `%`/`"`, kill `x`, new-window `c`** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/config.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/mod.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_attach.rs`, `docs/MUX.md`; feature `attach`): tmux muscle memory now works over an attach. After the prefix: `%` sends `split-window -t <focused> -h` and `"` sends `split-window -t <focused>` (the bare/default direction); both land the pump on the NEW pane — the daemon's reply body IS the fresh pane id, and the landing rides the same select-then-refresh contract every switch follows (passthrough: `select-pane` + `refresh-client` resync; render: focus + re-seed from the fresh `%layout-change`), so the redraw is the new pane's authoritative screen and the next keystrokes reach it. `x` sends `kill-pane -t <focused>`: the pump follows the window's surviving active pane (`list-panes` survivor lookup, the same land-on-the-successor move the switch chords make); killing the window's last pane engages the existing held-dead guard instead (typing dropped, `(exited ? — C-b r respawns)` cue — though the pane is gone, not held, so the honest exit from there is prefix `d`). `c` sends `new-window -t <focused-pane's-session>` (reply = the fresh window id), selects it, and attaches to its active pane. All four keys are settable per chord in the config file's `[client]` table (`split-right`, `split-down`, `kill-pane`, `new-window`; tmux spelling or a literal, parsed with the ONE `parse_prefix` grammar every other chord uses; defaults `%`, `"`, `x`, `c`), rebind live on the reload chord (the same pure `reload_client_chords` pass startup resolves them through — one grammar, one error shape; a malformed chord fails the attach or flashes the reload error). Chords matched by byte BEFORE the fixed command table, so a remapped key never collides with the static arms; an unbound key is consumed silently as before. Pinned headless by `split_right_chord_sends_split_window_and_lands_on_the_new_pane`, `split_down_chord_sends_the_vertical_split_spelling`, `kill_chord_sends_kill_pane_and_lands_on_the_survivor`, `kill_of_the_last_pane_engages_the_dead_guard`, `new_window_chord_follows_the_new_windows_active_pane`, `chord_override_remaps_the_split_chord`, and `management_chord_override_errors_on_malformed_spelling` (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/mod.rs`) plus `management_chords_parse_route_and_error` (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/config.rs`); PTY-level by `split_chord_lands_the_client_on_the_new_pane` and `kill_chord_lands_the_client_on_the_survivor` (`crates/par-mux/tests/mux_attach.rs`, unix). Commit `4e868aa`.
- **par-mux `attach --mode render`: visible cursor, obvious focus indicator, and a host-background frame fill** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_attach.rs`, `docs/MUX.md`, feature `attach`): the three render-mode gaps the owner's manual pass reported. **Cursor:** each frame now places the host cursor at the FOCUSED pane's tracked cell — the pane emulator's cursor position mapped through the pane's rect origin and the client scroll offset — emitting CUP + DECSCUSR (`CSI Ps SP q`, the pane's tracked shape; blink variants keep blinking) + DECTCEM (`CSI ?25 h/l`): a pane that hid its cursor keeps it hidden, and a scrolled-off-live view (wheel / prefix-[) hides the host cursor entirely, since the live cell is not on screen; the session re-emits only on a state change or after a flushed frame (whose per-cell CUPs moved the host cursor). **Focus indicator:** the focused pane's boundary dividers draw bright-cyan (indexed 14) plus bold — the shipped bold/dim-only highlight was indistinguishable on the owner's terminal — while unfocused dividers stay dim with the default foreground. **Background fill:** every frame cell carries the host terminal's resolved background before painting (`PaneRenderer::set_background`, dark `rgb:0/0/0` default matching the handshake's unprobed client-colors default), so unwritten cells — rows below the layout, short history lines, wide-char spacers — render as background instead of the ratatui default the buffer was born with (the light-grey bands a dark-theme host showed); painted cells keep the core grid's own bg. The OSC 10/11 probe plugs into `set_background` once `conn.rs` reports a resolved host background. Pinned by `focused_cursor_maps_cell_plus_origin_and_hides_when_scrolled`, `focused_cursor_carries_the_tracked_decscusr_shape`, `focused_divider_carries_accent_vs_dim_unfocused`, `frame_fills_every_cell_with_the_configured_background`, and `frame_replaces_cursor_after_flush_and_quiets_when_idle` (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`); the PTY-level screen-reconstruction helper skips the new cursor-placement sequences (`crates/par-mux/tests/mux_attach.rs`). Commit `7cf09ac`.
- **par-mux configuration file with live reload** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/config.rs` new, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/command/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/dispatch/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/server/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/mod.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/src/bin/par_mux/main.rs`, `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_cli.rs`, `docs/MUX.md`; feature `mux-bin`/`attach`): `par-mux` now reads `<config dir>/par-mux/config.toml` (or `$PAR_MUX_CONFIG`) for client defaults (attach `prefix`, default `mode`, the `reload` chord) and daemon defaults (`socket`, `state-dir`, `pane-endpoints`, `expose-control-socket`), with flags > env > file > built-in defaults precedence. Three reload entry points, no restart: the `reload-config` control command (no args; one reply line per `[daemon]` setting — `unchanged:`/`restart-required:`; the file speaks only for settings it states, so a daemon started with one-shot `--socket`/`--state-dir` flags stays quiet), the attach reload chord (default `C-b C-r`, itself configurable as `[client] reload`; rebinds prefix/reload LIVE in both passthrough and render mode, flashes `config reloaded`/the error on the status row, and sends `reload-config` so the daemon-side report rides the same keypress — the chord's key is matched by byte, so it never collides with prefix `r`/respawn), and `--gen-config [--force]` (writes the effective settings as TOML, refusing to overwrite without `--force`; `PAR_MUX_CONFIG` redirects the path — the tests never touch the user's real config dir). A present-but-broken config file warns-and-defaults at startup (the state-file D3.2 rule) but is an `%error` on reload: an edit mistake surfaces, never silently ignored. Startup-only in v1 (reported restart-required): `client.mode` (needs a re-attach), `daemon.socket`, `daemon.state-dir` (the persist worker's path is fixed at bind — the spec's tentative LIVE claim for state-dir did not hold and is documented startup-only), `daemon.pane-endpoints`/`expose-control-socket`. Pinned by the parse/precedence/round-trip/chord-parsing suites in `crates/par-mux/src/mux/config.rs` (10 tests), the dispatch report tests (`reload_config_reports_restart_required_per_changed_setting`, `reload_config_without_an_applied_copy_errors`, `reload_config_rejects_positionals` in `crates/par-mux/src/mux/dispatch/`), the live-daemon CLI round-trips (`reload_config_command_reaches_a_live_daemon`, `gen_config_writes_effective_config_and_refuses_overwrite` in `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_cli.rs`), and the live-rebind unit test (`reload_chord_rebinds_prefix_and_sends_reload_config` in `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/mod.rs`: the NEW prefix detaches after the chord, the old one forwards, `reload-config` on the wire). Commit `86f0782`.
- **par-mux `list-windows -a` and bounded client queries** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/command/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/dispatch/windows.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/status.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/client.rs`, `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_targets.rs`, `docs/MUX.md`; ARC-125): a new `all` feature token and flag returns `$S @N <marker> <name>` rows for every session's windows in one round trip, sorted by session id, so the attach status refresh finds a window's owning session in one query instead of one per session (older daemons fall back to the per-session scan, reusing the owner's reply). **Rust API:** `MuxCommand::ListWindows` gains an `all` field (the enum is not `#[non_exhaustive]`, so exhaustive matches need the field), and `MuxClient::send_checked_timeout` / `AttachConn::send_checked_timeout` bound any query.
- **par-mux `attach`: ship-gate integration coverage for resize re-fit and daemon zoom (render mode)** (`crates/par-mux/tests/mux_attach.rs`, `docs/MUX.md`, `docs/MANUAL-PASS.md` new, feature `attach`): two new PTY-level tests close the render-mode coverage gaps. `render_mode_host_resize_refits_window_layout_and_status_row` resizes the harness PTY master mid-session (the SIGWINCH shape) and asserts the full re-fit chain: the client reports the new grid with the content height, the daemon re-divides the window (`pane-info` ground truth at the new geometry), the renderer re-seeds both panes and repaints them at their re-divided rects, and the status row survives the repaint flood at the host's new bottom row (screen reconstructed from the client's per-cell diff stream). `render_mode_daemon_zoom_shows_single_pane_and_unzoom_restores_split` zooms the focused pane with the daemon's `resize-pane -Z`, asserts the visible-layout re-render (the zoomed pane's content repaints, the true layout keeps both panes under the window), then unzooms and asserts the hidden pane's restore repaint plus `capture-pane` ground truth through the round-trip. The test harness keeps its PTY master so tests can drive host-side resizes. `docs/MANUAL-PASS.md` is the human checklist for the ship gate's manual integration criterion: exact commands to build, start a daemon, seed sessions, and attach in both modes, what to eyeball per terminal (fidelity / resize / mouse / scroll / status bar / zoom / detach cleanliness) in Ghostty, iTerm2, Terminal.app, and over SSH, with a results table. Commit `747172f` (with the test-binding fix `547cd9e`).
- **par-mux `attach`: Phase B status bar and prefix-[ scroll mode (render mode)** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/status.rs` new, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_attach.rs`, `docs/MUX.md`, feature `attach`): the render-mode bottom row is now the full ratatui status bar — sessions (the shown one bold), the shown session's windows (active bold and `*`-marked), the focused pane's title, and the agent roster as `agent:state` chips — painted into a one-row buffer and flushed as a per-cell diff so a status change writes only the changed cells. The state is the throttled re-query the client contract teaches: `%agent-state-changed` / `%agent-released` / `%agent-telemetry-changed` / `%sessions-changed` (plus renames and active-window moves) mark it stale, and one refresh per event burst re-runs `list-sessions` / targeted `list-windows` / `pane-title` / `list-agents`; when the shown window's session is gone, the view ends cleanly (the `%sessions-changed` client contract). `prefix [` enters a keyboard scroll viewport over the focused pane's client scrollback (NOT tmux copy-mode — no search/copy/selection): the view holds against pane output (offset clamps to the new extent instead of snapping), moves with Up/Down (line), PgUp/PgDn (page), Home (history top), and exits with q / Enter / End snapping back to live; keys never reach the pane while it is up, and the status bar shows a bold `[scroll +N]` cue. Session/window switching reuses the passthrough dispatch semantics: prefix `o`/`n`/`p`/`(`/`)` are daemon-side selects followed by a full renderer re-seed (fresh layout report + per-pane replays) and a status re-query. The daemon window renders into the rows above the status row, so size reports carry the content height and a host resize repaints the row with the same redraw discipline as the pane frames. Pinned by the status unit suite in `status.rs` (composition facts, bold marking, scroll cue, truncation, row diffing, tail erasure), the renderer scroll-viewport tests in `render.rs` (hold-against-output, exit snap, no-scrollback refusal, history painting), and the PTY-level `render_mode_status_bar_shows_sessions_and_updates_on_agent_changes` + `render_mode_prefix_bracket_enters_scroll_mode_and_q_exits` (`crates/par-mux/tests/mux_attach.rs`). Commit `d4c05b6` (the third Listener-import gating in `0eb8a9f`).
- **par-mux `attach`: Phase B input router (render mode)** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/input.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, `crates/par-mux/src/bin/par_mux/main.rs`, feature `attach`): `par-mux attach --mode render` is now CLI-selectable (passthrough stays the default and an unknown `--mode` value falls back to it), and the render session's stdin is routed rather than forwarded. An incremental hand-rolled stdin tokenizer (`mux::attach::input::InputParser` — crossterm's parser is crate-private and its reader would race the client's own stdin thread) splits the host's byte stream into plain runs (forwarded verbatim behind the prefix scan), decodable key sequences, and SGR mouse reports. Key sequences re-encode against the focused pane's tracked input state via the shared `keyboard::encode_key` — DECCKM application cursor, kitty flags, modifyOtherKeys, exactly what the pane's replay + `%output` set — so a pane running vim gets `ESC O A` for Up while a plain shell gets `ESC [ A`. Mouse: the host captures SGR+motion tracking; a click locates the pane in the Phase B layout rects, focuses it (locally and daemon-side `select-pane`), and forwards a pane-relative re-encoded SGR report only when that pane owns mouse tracking (its emulator tracks the pane's own DECSET 1000/1002/1003); drag and release forward the same path. Wheel: over a pane that does NOT own mouse mode, the wheel scrolls the CLIENT's view into the pane's scrollback (3 lines/notch, clamped, painted from scrollback with the live screen shifted down; keystrokes snap back to live); over an owning pane it forwards pane-relative. Full kitty keyboard forwarding (negotiating flags, CSI u re-encode) is deferred to a later Phase B card and noted in docs/MUX.md. Pinned by the parser suite in `input.rs` (10 tests), the renderer router tests in `render.rs` (scrollback paint, mouse-ownership gating, rect lookup, DECCKM re-encode through the pane emulator), and the PTY-level `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_attach.rs` render-mode tests (arrow re-encoding verified against the pane's own capture, wheel scrollback, mouse forwarding pane-relative through a live daemon). Commit `68373f2` (status-row redraw after startup repaint floods and host resizes in `61cff0d`).
- **par-mux `attach`: Phase B layout parser and pane renderer** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/layout.rs`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/render/`, feature `attach`, now with ratatui 0.30): the rendering foundation the full attach TUI builds on, behind a mode seam that leaves Phase A untouched. The layout parser maps a `%layout-change` triple (`<layout> <visible-layout> <flags>`) to absolute per-pane rects — the `Z` flag renders the visible (zoomed) layout, empty triples (bare `%window-add`) parse as no panes, malformed strings error with a byte offset. Golden tests over real-tmux layout strings plus round-trips against the daemon's own `LayoutTree::render`/`geometry` pin the same leaf order `list-panes -t` reports. The renderer mirrors every visible pane in its own core `Terminal` fed by the `refresh-client -t` replays and `%output` bytes, maps cells to a ratatui `Buffer` (truecolor passthrough, SGR attributes, wide-char spacers marked skip in the diff, combining marks joined into one symbol), draws dividers with a focused-pane highlight (UTF-8 box drawing with an ACS `| - +` fallback), and damage-diffs frames at a 16 ms cadence — an output flood between two frames coalesces into one diff, pinned by the flood test. Passthrough stays the default (`run`); `run_with_mode(AttachMode::Render)` selects the renderer. Pinned by the unit suites in both modules and `render_mode_layout_and_pane_replay_match_daemon_ground_truth` (`crates/par-mux/tests/mux_attach.rs`: a live daemon's split layout string parses to the daemon's rects and each pane's replay reproduces its `capture-pane` ground truth). Commit `953ae0e` (Windows import gating in `9d7c678`).
- **par-mux `attach`: Phase A passthrough client** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/`, `crates/par-mux/src/bin/par_mux/main.rs`, feature `attach`): `par-mux attach [-t TARGET] [--prefix KEY]` puts one pane fullscreen in your terminal — the host terminal is the pane's VT emulator. The client runs the documented handshake, resolves the target (pane id; window/session via the targeted list queries; no `-t` = newest session's newest pane), writes the pane's screen-restore replay to stdout verbatim, and forwards `%output` raw. Stdin flows back in chunked `send-keys -H` (~512-byte chunks) behind a `C-b` prefix (`--prefix` overrides): `d` detaches (terminal restored, exit 0, pane keeps running), `o`/arrows cycle panes, `n`/`p` and `(`/`)` switch windows/sessions, `r` respawns a held-dead pane — each switch a daemon-side `select-*` plus `refresh-client` resync redraw. A DECSTBM-reserved bottom status row shows session, pane title, agent count, and an `(exited N)` cue. On `%exit`/socket close the client does one reconnect-and-requery before reporting the loss. Mouse tracking and graphics protocols work in passthrough (pane bytes flow through, host reports flow back). Pinned by the unit suite in `crates/par-mux/src/mux/attach/mod.rs` (FakeDaemon wire framing) and `crates/par-mux/tests/mux_attach.rs` (the client under a real PTY: byte fidelity, prefix detach, resync switching, clean restore). Commit `ff46b96`, building on the scaffolding commit `663be0c` (with the Windows-side stdin-thread and import fixes `92a35c6` and `bd5ebf8`).
- **par-mux `refresh-client` accepts the `-t`-less size-report form** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/command/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/dispatch/`): the attach handshake's `refresh-client -C WxH -p WxH` (no `-t`) previously `%error`ed, so the size report never landed. `RefreshClient`'s pane is now optional: the target-less form is a pure size report — `-p` applies daemon-wide exactly as before and `-C` resizes the newest session's active window (the same stand-in bare `new-window` uses) — and never replays a pane screen; the replay stays bound to the `-t` form. Pinned by `parses_target_less_refresh_client_as_a_pure_size_report` (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/command/`), `target_less_refresh_client_sizes_the_newest_active_window`, `target_less_refresh_client_with_no_sessions_errors_cleanly`, and `target_less_refresh_client_never_replays_a_screen` (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/dispatch/`).
- **par-mux `list-windows -t <session>` and `list-panes -t <window>`** (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/command/`, `crates/par-mux/src/mux/dispatch/`): the attach path's tree queries. `list-windows -t` replies one line per window of that session in session order as the fixed positional shape `@N <marker> <name>` (`*` marks the session's active window, `-` the rest; the name is the line remainder, so a spaced name survives). `list-panes -t` replies one line per pane of that window in layout leaf order as `%N <leaf> <marker>` — `leaf` is the deterministic index a client maps tmux layout-string leaves to pane ids by, and the same marker marks the window's active pane. Both queries accept typed ids and names; the bare global forms keep their exact pre-existing shapes (`%N` lines, `@N: name` lines), so existing clients are untouched. `list-commands` advertises the new `targeted` feature token on both rows. Fixed shapes, no `-F` — the T4.E decision. Pinned by `targeted_list_windows_and_list_panes_reconstruct_the_tree` (`crates/par-mux/tests/mux_targets.rs`) and `parses_targeted_list_windows_and_list_panes` (`crates/par-mux/src/mux/command/`). Commit `ed94c97`.
